1 
1104 環球水泥 ( UCC )

報告日期： 2026 / 08 / 18 研 究 員：林奕頎 (Yichi Lin)  
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.公告 2Q / 202 6 稅後 EPS + 0.71 元， 本業衰退，依賴業外挹注  
環泥 公告 2Q / 2026 財報 單季 營收 YoY – 9%，即使環泥已經改
採 進 口 水 泥 ， 降 低 混 凝 土 成 本 ， 但2Q / 2026 單 季 營 利 率 + 
14.5% ，仍低於 2Q / 2025 同期的  + 15.2% ，2Q/ 2026 單季營業利
益 YoY – 13% ； 依賴 2Q / 2026 認列較高額股利收入 （過去年
度，多數股利認在 3Q，2Q / 2026 則不同） ，拉高業外收淨收支； 
此外，2Q / 2026 單季稅率  9.0%，低於 2Q / 2025 同期的  13.4% ； 
因此， 2Q / 2026 單季稅後 EPS + 0.71 元， YoY + 25% ，高於 2Q / 
2025 同期的  + 0.57 元。  
二.營收 衰退， 凸顯產業困境，長期下滑趨勢日益明顯  
環泥 2Q / 2026 單季  三大營收 YoY 都明顯下滑：  混凝土營收
YoY  - 18%（連續 2 季下滑） ；  水泥 YoY  - 17%；（連續 7 季下
滑） ；  石膏板 YoY - 3%（連續 5 季下滑） 。 環泥三大營收下
滑，凸顯  台灣不動產景氣下滑  及 台商建廠 潮退燒  的困境。
未來，產業 的長期下滑趨勢 ，恐將日益明顯。  
三.環泥 改採進口水泥 ； 中南部  殺價競爭  漸趨激烈  
環泥 2025 年 11 月起，全面 改採較便宜的進口水泥作為混凝土
原料， 降低混凝土成本 。 長期來看，將加速水泥混凝土整體產
業獲利 向下滑落；  台灣的水泥及混凝土產能都明顯過剩 ，主要
是透過 業者自律 ，避免嚴重價格競爭，維持較佳獲利；  目前 ，
環泥及國產 改採進口水泥 ，台泥及亞泥 只能 進行價格戰， 以搶
回市場佔有率， 最終會將價格 水準 壓到讓使用進口水泥的混凝
土業者無利可圖 。  依據新聞，台灣區預拌混凝土公會指出，
2025 年台灣預拌混凝土總銷售量  4,551 萬立方米；  估 2026 年
將跌破  4,000 萬立方米， YoY 下滑超過  1 成； 目前  台南、高
雄、台中、彰化  等 中南部地區，已經出現價格競爭 ，價格下
降 10% ~ 15% ； 凸顯  進口水泥增加、國內建案開工率下降、
高科技建廠高 峰結束 等 利空，衝擊轉 強。   環 泥 的市場 偏重
南部，已成殺價重心地區， 過去依賴業者自律的協同模式逐漸
被打破，回歸市場競爭，長期的殺價 競爭 恐將成為常態 。 
四.建議： 產業競爭日益激烈，本 業獲利下滑趨勢明顯 ；展望保守  
環泥 2Q/2026 財報， 顯示 三大 本業的下滑趨勢，日益明顯 ； 展
望未來，建案開工量減少、台商建廠 潮退燒、 進口水泥 競爭 ，
將使  水泥  及 混凝土  殺價競爭  日益激烈 ； 宏遠認為， 環泥
獲利 處在 長期下滑 趨勢 ； 長期 展望保守 。
投資評等：區間操作 
操作區間：23 ~ 28 元 
  10 ~ 12 X 2026 年稅後 EPS

參考價位：26.75 元 
  P/E：11.3 X 2026 年稅後 EPS 
  P/E：13.8 X 2027 年稅後 EPS 
  2026 / 08 / 17 收盤

2Q / 2026 單季 三大本業衰退： 
混凝土營收：連 2 季 YoY 下滑 
水泥  營收：連 7 季 YoY 下滑 
石膏板營收：連 5 季 YoY 下滑

水泥及混凝土 
同業協同 逐漸被打破 
價格戰力道逐漸轉強

一旦，局面失控 
水泥跟混凝土 
出現 斷崖式跌價 
屆時，獲利預估必須大幅下修

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
環泥營收來源 （100% 台灣）： 
202 1 年  全年 ：混凝土 65% ； 水泥 21% ； 石膏板 13% ；  
其他 1%。 
2022 年  全年 ：混凝土 65% ； 水泥 21% ； 石膏板 13% ； 
其他 1%。 
2023 年  全年 ：混凝土 64% ； 水泥 22% ； 石膏板 14% ； 
其他 0%。 
2024 年  全年 ：混凝土 64% ； 水泥 22% ； 石膏板 14% ； 
其他 0%。 
2025 年  全年 ：混凝土 67% ； 水泥 19% ； 石膏板 12% ； 
其他 2%。 
2026 年  1H ：混凝土 60% ； 水泥 19% ； 石膏板 13% ； 其
他 8%。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1.2026 年混凝土營收明顯下滑 ，產業展望保守看待  
環泥 ，2026 年 1H，6 成營收來自於  混凝土收入。

過去三年， 2023 年 ~ 2025 年， 環泥 的混凝土營收，分別為：  
NT$ 50.02 億元、 51.27 億元、 52.94 億元，呈現 逐年小幅成長 。

但進入 2026 年，環泥混凝土營收 （參閱圖 1）： 
1Q / 2026 單季 ：NT$ 10.73 億元， YoY – 19% 。 
2Q / 2026 單季： NT$ 10.70 億元， YoY – 18% 。

2026 年連續 2 季，混凝土營收 YoY 出現明顯下滑；  
同時， 2Q 的混泥土營收  低於  1Q 的農曆過年淡季 ； 
環泥的混凝土營收大減，凸顯產業下行的狀況日益明顯。

由於台灣房地產，成屋過剩日益嚴重，預售案銷售不佳及建案開
工率減少，加上，台 商返鄉建廠潮明顯降溫， 台灣的水泥及混凝
土產業的下行 ，很可能還會持續很長的時間 。

在水泥及混凝土產業下行的狀況下， 進口水泥的大量引進 ， 更讓
產業過剩及同業競爭加重 ， 水泥及混凝土業者獲利很可能 出現 斷
崖式減少 ，成為投資人的風險所在，投資人應提前避開。  
投資評等：買進

2026.08.18 
吳昆潮 C00269@ibfs.com.tw 
1513.TT 中興電 
 財報優於預期，在手訂單充裕

[財務資訊更新]   
   公司簡介 
中興電是台灣電機設備製造商，為台灣電力基礎建設的重要供應商，重電產品以直接
銷售方式售予台電公司、公共設施事業及各大工廠，台電是單一最大客戶。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 198 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】買進，195 
【大盤指數/股價】45857 / 171

➢ 2Q26 EPS 2.56 元，獲利優於預期 
中興電 2Q26 營收 75.33 億元，QoQ+16.06%，YoY+6.03%，稅後淨
利 12.68 億元，QoQ+35.52%，YoY+34.65%，EPS 2.56 元，優於投研
部預期，主要受惠台電強韌電網計畫、3-1 期離岸風電及半導體擴廠，
產品組合優於預期 ， 毛利率回升至28.44%， 優於投研部預期的26.15%。 
➢ 上修資本支出以因應產能需求 
中興電因應目前產能滿載 、 且部分長單能見度直達2031 年的暢旺需求 ，
2026 年的資本支出上修至 15 至 20 億元 ， 除了用來鞏固GIS 升級測試 、
生質能設備代理與 BOT 案等綠能本業外，並投入 AI 系統與設備建置，
中興電預計透過導入「自動化焊接」與「AI 報價系統」雙管齊下，精進
製造與商務效率，目標在 2026 年將整體產能拉升 15%到 20%，全力消
化手頭訂單。 
➢ 截至 3 月底在手訂單達 431 億元，營運穩健 
中興電在氣體絕緣開關具絕對的領 先優勢，在台電與非台電體系市佔率
達 80%及 65%。隨著全球能源轉型與 AI 算力崛起，中興電全面受惠於
「台電強韌電網計畫」 、離岸風電與半導體海內外擴廠；同時，國際大廠
積極建置 AI 與 IDC 資料中心，更引爆龐大的重電設備剛性需求，中興
電憑藉壓倒性的技術實力與市場份額，將全面受惠海內外建設紅利，持
續驅動 GIS 業績穩定成長，其中 GIS 已取得國內晶圓半導體大廠認證，
預計 4Q26 開始出貨。截至 2026 年 3 月底，中興電在重電事業新接訂
單金額 63 億元，在手訂單累計 431 億元，而台電強韌電網計畫及半導
體擴廠專案的延續性佳，未來中興電接單及營運動能穩健。 
➢ 預估 2027 年 EPS 10.21 元，維持買進評等，目標價 198 元 
投研部預估中興電 2026 年營收 292.29 億元，YoY+6.49%，預估稅後
淨利 46.07 億元，YoY+15.52%，稅後 EPS 9.32 元；預估 2027 年營收
317.90 億元，YoY+8.76%，稅後淨利 50.45 億元，YoY+9.50%，EPS 
10.21 元。國內台電穩定釋單，訂單能見度佳，且 GIS 設備正式打入國
內半導體大廠供應鏈 ， 有利評價拉升 ， 目前PE 16.7X， 位於PE 15X~25X
區間下緣，維持買進評等，目標價 198 元(2027F PER 19X)。

公司簡介 
中興電是台灣電機設備製造商，為台灣電力基礎建設的重要供應商，重電產品以直接
銷售方式售予台電公司、公共設施事業及各大工廠，台電是單一最大客戶，佔中興電
營收比重約 50%，其中 345kV 氣體絕緣開關設備(GIS)，是國內唯一獲得台電認證的
本土廠商，而69kV 以上高壓開關在國內的市佔率達 70％，變電所的統包工程市佔率
37%。中興電主要以國內市場為主，內銷佔營收比重約 91%。

截至 3 月底在手訂單達 431 億元，合約負債金額維持高檔 
中興電在氣體絕緣開關(GIS)具有絕對的領先優勢， 在台電與非台電體系市佔率達80%
及 65%。隨著全球能源轉型與 AI 算力崛起， 中興電全面受惠於 「台電強韌電網計畫」 、
離岸風電與半導體海內外擴廠；同時，國際大廠積極建置 AI 與 IDC 資料中心，更引
爆龐大的重電設備剛性需求，中興電憑藉壓倒性的技術實力與市場份額，將全面受惠
海內外建設紅利，持續驅動 GIS 業績穩定成長，其中 GIS 已取得國內晶圓半導體大廠
認證，預計 4Q26 開始出貨。截至 2026 年 3 月底，中興電在重電事業新接訂單金額
63 億元，在手訂單累計 431 億元；至 6 月底，公司合約負債金額 89 億元，維持高
檔，台電強韌電網計畫及半導體擴廠專案的延續性佳，未來中興電接單及營運動能穩
健。

和成 (1810 TT)  Buy

無人機載具出貨台系廠商。  
投資建議 ：軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛浴系統穩定成
長，預估 2026 年軍工營收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾
15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億
元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會
編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建
議 Buy ，目標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR 1.18X) 。  
精密陶瓷事業，佈局國防領域，推出 RF3 等級抗彈板 ：積極佈
局國防領域。現階段 RF2 等級抗彈板持續出貨中，通路經銷商
等期望公司加速最高等級 RF3 抗彈板研發並供貨。因供給相對
有限，需求提升，預估該產品毛利表現佳。  
強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具： 開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈，且 2025 年已開始出貨。
無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開
發無人機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型
無人機機身之碳纖維複合材料，正放量出貨中。  
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案： 08/14/2026 立法院
正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統
籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，
總金額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨： 2026 年
預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍
工營收比重約 10%，2027 年預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，
且 2027 年軍工營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026
年營收 46.83 億元， YoY+2.76% ，稅後純益 12.44 億元，
YoY+8,597.62% ，稅後 EPS 4.12 元； 2027 年營收 52.36 億元，
YoY+11.82% ，稅後純益 2.74 億元， YoY-77.98% ，稅後 EPS 0.91
元。

2 群益投顧 
  和成 ESG ：  
和成 HCG 集團於 08/12/2025 上傳申報並公告 2024 年度 ESG 永續報告書。
永續經營方面 ， 針對溫室氣體報告以 2024 年度為基準年自主揭露營運概況 ，
以降低溫室氣體排放為目標，汰換柴 油堆高機為電動堆高機，預計 2027 年
底前更新柴油設備 4 台為電動堆高機，減少碳排放量約 18 公噸 /年。以善盡
企業社會責任為己任，榮獲第 8 屆台灣陶藝金陶獎，並舉辦后里森林復育計
畫。節水衛浴系統方面，取得 2025 年金點新秀設計獎，並馳援花蓮馬太鞍
溪堰塞湖災害援助，提供馬桶、臉盆及水龍頭等衛浴設備， 2.5~6 折優惠；
品牌永續方面，旗下包含和成三廠，以及台北、台中、台南、高雄等 4 大展
示中心，並於馬尼拉設立旗艦展示中心。  
公司簡介：  
和成成立於 12/09/1961 ，並於 10/14/1991 掛牌上市。主要產品包括各種衛浴
設備及周邊產品生產銷售，並積極佈局精密陶瓷事業、強化碳纖複合材料等
新材料事業。生產基地有桃園、新北、中國江蘇及菲律賓馬尼拉。  
2026 年超配現金股利：  
經董事會通過，和成 2026 年配發 2025 年度每股現金股利 0.2 元，現金配發
率達 400% 。  
表一、和成近 10 年 (2016~2025) 股利政策

資料來源：和成、 CMoney ，群益投顧彙整  
2025 年推出新衛浴產品，持續深耕本業：  
持續推出新型衛浴產品，主要致力於開發 UB 整體衛浴 -模塊裝配式衛浴，
公司主要負責安裝防水盤、壁板及天花板、衛浴設備、燈具、排氣扇等產品。
UB 整體衛浴以同層排水為主要設計更新。 2025 年推出 HCG 智慧型超級馬
桶及精湛型超級馬桶、薄型系列龍頭。  
精密陶瓷事業，佈局國防領域，推出 RF3 等級抗彈板：  
05/2025 和成參與台北國際安全科技展覽會、台北國際防火防災運用展，大
秀其國防產品 -RF2、RF3 抗彈板。顯示公司於精密陶瓷事業端積極推廣人身

3 群益投顧 
級抗彈板持續出貨中， RF3 等級抗彈板於會展後詢問度大幅上揚，通路經銷
商等期望公司加速最高等級 RF3 抗彈板研發並供貨。因供給相對有限，需
求提升，預估該產品毛利表現佳。  
圖二、和成抗彈板

資料來源：和成法說簡報  
強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具：  
和成除持續深耕本業衛浴系統外，為強化轉型動能，積極開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈， 2023 年陸續生產，且 2025 年已開始
出貨。無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開發無人
機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型無人機機身之碳纖維
複合材料，正放量出貨中，且和成亦提供高純度氧化鋁、碳化矽等陶瓷產品，
配合雷虎生產無人艇引擎之遮蔽熱源及抗雷達偵測設備，且處於測試過程。
為打入半導體擴建廠房商機，亦提供無塵室格子樑及鋪設於店面之導管，以
SMC/ 熱壓成型製程製造。受惠日本大廠 TOTO 打入半導體供應鏈，可望為
具備精密陶瓷及高溫燒結技術，身兼半導體級製程關鍵材料技術之和成營
運添磚加瓦，可運用於半導體產業與國防裝備上，增添打入半導體設備關鍵
零組件供應鏈想像。  
圖四、和成碳纖維複合材料

資料來源：和成法說簡報

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 17 日

4 群益投顧 
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案：  
08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業
發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，總金
額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。外貿協會董事長更表示無人
機產業馬上要起飛，且將持續協助相關業者開拓海外營運版圖。無人機事業
相關供應商，包括雷虎、漢翔、中光電、長榮航太等，而和成主要提供上述
廠商無人機、無人艇之碳纖維複合材料。  
1Q26 認列搬遷補償利益 14.33 億元，單季獲利超越 2025 年：  
和成 1Q26 營收 10.35 億元， QoQ-7.74% 、YoY-8.14% ；營費毛利 1.80 億元，
毛利率約 17.36% ；業外收支 14.13 億元，主因認列蘇州潤淞開發建設之搬
遷補償利益 14.33 億元、租金股利及權利金等收入 1,850 萬元，以及其他利
益與損失 4,198.7 萬元；稅後純益 10.98 億元， QoQ+31,877.38% 、 YoY 虧轉
盈，稅後 EPS 3.63 元。  
2Q26 受惠軍工產品出貨增加，毛利率大幅提升至 25.40% ：  
和成 2Q26 營收 11.47 億元， QoQ+10.81% 、 YoY-8.28% ；營費毛利 2.91 億
元，毛利率約 25.40% ，主因軍工產品出貨量增加，營收比重同步提升；惟
業外收支因 1Q26 基期較高，大幅下滑，使得稅後虧損 6 百萬元， QoQ 盈轉
虧、 YoY 盈轉虧，稅後 EPS -0.02 元。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨：  
2026 年主要營運動能，包括衛浴本業、無人機載具及半導體晶圓載具、軌
道工程產品開發等。預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，將持續強化整體衛浴
UB 產品，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍工營收比重約 10%。2027 年受惠營
建產業可望落底逐步回升，預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，且 2027 年軍
工產品營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026 年營收 46.83 億元，
YoY+2.76% ，稅後純益 12.44 億元， YoY+8,597.62% ，稅後 EPS 4.12 元；
2027 年營收 52.36 億元， YoY+11.82% ，稅後純益 2.74 億元， YoY-77.98% ，
稅後 EPS 0.91 元。  
投資建議：  
軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛 浴系統穩定成長，預估 2026 年軍工營
收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾 15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通
過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及
行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建議 Buy ，目
標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR1.18X) 。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 4

AI server: Robust demand outlook, Rubin generation ramp on schedule

### Page 5

Robust AI server demand outlook supported by strong Capex spending
 We model 45% AI chip shipment CAGR in 2025-28.
 GPU server demand remains robust this year on resilient US CSP AI spending and pent-up Neocloud/enterprise demand.
 We see longer demand visibility into next year on strong demand outlook and sustained U.S. CSP capex spending growth.
 We forecast a 60% CAGR for AI ASIC chip shipments in 2025-28, driven by strong AWS Trainium/Google TPU demand.

AI server shipment forecast
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates for all years, including historical. Note: Total AI server includes AI ASIC + high-end GPU AI servers.

### Page 6

Upbeat 2026 AI capex outlook, extended visibility into 2027
 U.S. hyperscalers raised 2026 capex outlook and provided positive commentary on 2027.
 JPM U.S. hardware team now forecast +80%/+50% YoY growth of datacenter capex in 2026/27, vs. previous estimates of +63%/+40% YoY,
which is the third raise this year. We see ~10% upside to the estimates after the recent updated outlook for leading hyperscalers.
 While negative FCF among leading hyperscalers over the next few quarters is inevitable, we see a few positive indicators to support continued AI

investments, including robust core businesses, strong AI demand indicators, and decent returns of AI businesses with continued improvement.
 Datacenter capex from Tier 2 providers (e.g. emerging neocloud and other data center-related companies) is now becoming a much more significant
cohort.
 We expect the group to grow capex by 65%/39% YoY to US$90bn/US$130bn in 2026/2027.
 We estimate US$5.5tn of AI capex over 2026-30, with US$4.1tn funded through debt. We expect the IG corporate debt market to finance more than

US$2.1tn of data center investments over the next five years

### Page 8

90% of APAC respondents on board with AI, with upside in spending in the near future
 JPM Asia Pacific Artificial Intelligence Implementation Survey 2026 (link) suggests that AI investment is already mainstream, with 88% of APAC
respondents reporting AI expenditure over the last 12 months, while only ~3% said they had not invested and ~7% were unsure. Key findings include:
 AI investment is mainstream and growing fast, but leadership is uneven.

 AI stated value case is productivity-led but with a growth mindset; so far cost reduction is secondary.
 Execution capability will increasingly separate AI winners; most respondents were optimistic about profit implications.
 Average AI spend is expected to rise from 4.5% of expenses plus capex over the last 12 months to 5.8% over the next 12 months.
AI expenditure/investment (last 12 months) by region

### Page 9

Nvidia GPU roadmap: Rubin on schedule, Kyber architecture facing challenges
 Vera Rubin system ramps up on schedule in late 3Q to 4Q26
 Our research indicates limited impact on the Vera Rubin ramp schedule from the recent leading PCB maker yield issue.
 We estimate 70-80k NVL72 rack shipments this year, comprising 60-65k GB300 rack shipments and ~10k VR rack shipments.
 We continue to forecast ~1.5mn Rubin chip production this year (board level output), but expect only ~10k VR NVL72 rack shipments due to

production cycle time and component shortages.
 We expect 85-95k NVL72 rack shipments in 2027, with VR200 rack being the majority.
NVL72 ODM rack quarterly shipments (k units)
Nvidia GPU server roadmap
Source: J.P. Morgan, Company data.

### Page 10

Deep dive into NVL72 BOM cost: GPU and Memory to be the key cost driver
 We estimate the ODM price of the GB300/VR200 rack to be ~US$4.1/6.5mn, ~20%/90% higher than that of the GB200 server, primarily driven by
GPU and memory price increases and content upgrades in the networking components and power and thermal systems.
 We expect AI server ODM GMs to decline to 3.6%/2.7% of GB300/VR200 vs. 4.2% in GB200.
 However, we see MVA dollars (GP dollars per rack) increasing in VR200, due to a more complex server architecture and longer testing time.

 We estimate ~US$148k/ $175k for the MVA of GB300/VR200 vs. ~US$145k for GB200.
NVL72 BoM - Summary table
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates.

### Page 11

Secular ASIC server demand
 We forecast AI ASIC chips to grow 77%/88% YoY in 2026/2027, mainly driven by strong AWS Trainium and Google TPU demand.
 As AI inference activities scale, we expect more end customers to develop its own AI inference ASIC solutions, which could further expand the AI
ASIC server TAM.
JPM ASIC chip/server estimates

### Page 12

Consolidated market landscape in AI server ODM space
 Hon Hai and Quanta stand out as preferred picks in the NVL72 supply chain, while Celestica and Wiwynn lead among ASIC plays.
 While we see tier-2 ODMs (e.g. Pegatron, Compal) managing to break into the space, complex design capabilities, frequent product upgrades, and
high working capital requirements should keep the competitive landscape concentrated among a few major players.

### Page 13

General Servers: Multi-year strong demand driven by AI, key swing factor on supply constraints

### Page 14

Agentic AI CPUs becoming a new driver for server CPU demand
 As we migrate towards the era of agentic AI, focusing on test-time scaling (i.e. long thinking), CPUs are playing an increasingly important role in
orchestration, running tools and skills, storage, and security.
 For example, the Vera CPU not only sits in the NVL72 rack systems, but also serves as a standalone CPU across the entire Vera Rubin platform,
including storage servers and CPU servers.

 We attribute the strong general server demand this year (30-40% YoY growth on our estimates) primarily to the emerging demand for agentic AI.
Source: Nvidia.
Agent = LLM + Harness; CPU plays a critical role in the agentic AI ecosystem

### Page 15

Accelerating server CPU growth cycle on AI inference cycle
 We segment CPU demand into three buckets—(1) AI server headnode CPUs, (2) regular server CPUs, and (3) agentic-AI CPUs.
 As AI applications transition from training toward inference, we expect agentic-AI and AI headnode CPU demand to drive a “super” general server
CPU cycle.
 We forecast total server CPU shipments to grow from 26mn units in 2025 to 68mn by 2028 (38% CAGR) based on a top-down methodology,

triangulated with bottom-up estimates.
 We forecast a 74%/5%/155% CAGR of AI server headnode CPU/ regular server CPU/ agentic AI server CPU demand in 2025-28.
 We forecast a 53% CAGR of server CPU revenue TAM in 2025-2028, driven by the fast-growing server CPU demand and a ~10% CAGR of server
CPU ASP expansion.
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates. Gartner® data for 2024 total server CPU shipments. Global AI accelerator shipments are based on our CoWoS estimates by the semi team.

JPM server CPU forecast - top-down methodology

### Page 17

Super server cycle underpinned by the recent uptick in x86 CPU vendor revenue growth
 We have seen accelerating growth in Intel/AMD server datacenter CPU revenue growth in the past three quarters, driven by strong server demand.
 AMD also cast an upbeat long-term outlook for server CPU TAM (US$220bn by 2030 or ~50%+ CAGR in 2025-2030), which underpins our research
of the multi-year super server cycle across hyperscalers and the positive demand signals across the supply chain.

### Page 18

An AI-led multi-year general server upcycle
 1Q26 U.S. CSP server shipments were better-than-seasonal at up QoQ and the overall server shipments appear to have grown double-digit QoQ in
2Q26.
 Initial outlook indicates server shipments should continue to grow into 2H26, led by U.S. CSP server demand.
 We have picked up incremental positive signs in server demand over the past 3-6 months with order visibility extended into next year.
 Lotes and ASPEED’s order visibility has already extended into 2027. Memory vendors also collected 50-80% demand growth feedback from the

U.S. CSPs for next year. The key swing factor is supply chain constraints.
 We anticipate an accelerating shipment growth of 25% YoY next year, driven by accumulated backlog from this year and strong server demand.
 By customer type, we forecast 40-50% growth/ single digit decline to flattish for CSPs/enterprise server shipments in 2026/27, respectively.
 In the longer run, we expect server shipments to sustain at double digit growth in 2028-29, driven by the multi-year AI-led server demand.

Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

 We forecast ~45% CAGR for AI accelerator shipments based on

### Page 26

AI-driven general purpose server demand sets the stage for switching growth
 We expect general server shipments to accelerate over the next few years on AI-driven inference demand.
 We estimate CSP’s general server shipment growth of 40%/25%/20% in 2026/27/28.
 There could be upside risks to our general server shipment assumptions driven by fast-growing agentic AI demand.
 Over the past 2–3 years, CSPs have prioritized AI server investment and data center build-outs, while networking has lagged.

 Gartner® data shows ODM-direct server spending increased 207% / 127% in 2024/25 versus ODM direct switch spending growth of 18%/103%
over the same period.
 We expect CSPs to step up networking spending to reduce latency and improve data transmission rates, presenting huge growth
opportunities for whitebox Ethernet switches.

### Page 29

PCs: Unit downside in sight, but margin/value better than feared

### Page 30

Better-than-feared PC demand in 1H26 likely driven by pull-in orders, cautious on 2H26
PC demand
 1H26 PC shipments appear better-than-expected, likely due to continued Win10 replacement demand and the pull-in of orders ahead of the memory
price hikes.
 We are cautious on PC end demand in 2H26 due to pull-forward demand in 1H26, the negative price elasticity from key component cost hikes, and
late cycle of Win10 PC replacement.
 We anticipate a sub-seasonal shipment trend in 2H26 and forecast a 3% HoH decline for PC shipments.

 We forecast an 8% PC shipment decline this year, comprising 14%/4% consumer/commercial PC shipment decline.

### Page 32

Unit downside in sight, but PC brands’ margin/value better than feared
 Asian PC brands delivered better-than-expected PC margins in 1H26, driven by better-than-feared PC demand, continued product price hikes, and
product mix adjustments.
 Our research indicates 20-30% YoY growth of PC ASP in 1H26. This, along with the lower-cost PC inventory at hand, drove meaningful margin
expansion for brands.
 In contrast, we saw PC ODM margin dilution in 1H26 due to the ASP hikes, while profit dollars remain intact.

 Looking ahead, while we have seen weakening PC demand in 2H26, we expect PC brands margin trend likely to be better than feared due to
continued product mix adjustments with pricing actions.
 We expect tier one PC brands’ margins to be more resilient in 2H26 due to better supply chain capability.
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

consensus estimates.
PC brands PC OPM trend

### Page 33

NB BoM analysis supports a 20-30% PC product price hikes by brands in 1H26
 We estimate that the BoM cost of high-end NBs has increased by ~30% over the past year, mainly driven by the price hikes in memory, non-key
semis (analog ICs), software license fees, and other components (e.g. passive components, PCBs).
 We estimate memory cost to increase by US$200-250 after 3-5x DRAM/NAND price hikes over the past year, driving the memory as % of total

NB BoM to rise to 20-30% vs. HSD % a year ago.
 We also assume a 5% price hike for other components & non-key semis.
 The BoM cost increases have also driven PC brands to implement 20–30% price hikes in 1H26 to protect margins
 We believe mid to low range PCs could have a higher sensitivity to the memory BoM % increase given the lower PC price band.
 PC brands have mitigated the impact through spec downgrades and product mix adjustments.
Source: Gartner®, J.P. Morgan estimates.

NB BoM analysis

### Page 34

Graphics/Motherboards – Soft end demand in the absence of new product ramps
 We have seen weakening end demand for PC motherboard and VGA in 1H26 due to memory supply shortage and negative price elasticity from
product price hikes.
 Looking ahead, we expect a sub-seasonal shipment growth (~5% QoQ growth) for the top 3 MB/VGA vendor shipments in 3Q26 and continue to
forecast a double-digit shipment decline for the full year MB/VGA shipments.
 We see limited catalysts for the market given the lack of new product launches in the next six months.

 This, coupled with the memory-led end demand weakness, implies potential margin pressure for PC component brands.
Source: J.P. Morgan estimates, Company data.

### Page 36

Delta: Key beneficiary of secular AI datacenter power and thermal trend
 We believe Delta will be a major beneficiary of the fast-growing datacenter power supply TAM (JPMe +80% CAGR in 2025-28), driven by
 Rising power consumption of AI servers
 Non-PSU content increase from power rack adoption and HVDC material upgrades
 Delta possesses the leading position (40%+) in datacenter power (esp. in high-end market) and liquid cooling space.
 We model above-seasonal revenue growth in 3Q/4Q at +16%/+14% QoQ respectively, driven by order push-out from 2Q26, new AI ASIC project

ramp in 2H, 110kW power shelf / HVDC product ramp in 4Q, and product price hikes in late 3Q26.
 We anticipate a gradual margin recovery helped by product price hikes and favorable pricing terms for new products.
 We expect the stock to regain momentum over the next three months as datacenter related revenues re-accelerate. Stay OW.

Wiwynn: Well positioned across GPU, AI ASIC, and general servers

### Page 41

Lotes: Near-term GM headwind, eyes on strong server demand and AI optionality
 Near-term margin weighed down by raw material price increases, depletion of low-cost inventory, and ramp-up costs for new products.
 We expect stable GMs and a mild OPM rebound in 3Q26, with a more meaningful margin recovery in 1H27 as new product cycles ramp.
 We view general server demand intact and keep our forecast of 20–30% server shipment growth over 2025–28, despite near-term supply uncertainty.

 We believe AI products could bring potential upside to Lotes’ business, driven by SOCAMM / QD ramp and mass production of OE socket in 2H27.

### Page 57

What drives the ASP expansion of high-end datacenter switch?
 We estimate a ~150% ASP increase for 800G switches versus 400G switches, driven by
 2-3x higher Switch ASIC cost (Contributing ~70% of the total ASP increase)
 PCB / CCL Materials upgrade to M8 from M6+, 3-4x CCL content growth
 CPO adoption could also significantly increase the switch BoM and bodes well for ODM margins given the high complexity of
component integration (FAU, OE, shuffle box, etc.).

either a lack of a sufficient fundamental basis or for legal, regulatory or policy reasons. The previous rating and, if applicable, the price target, no longer should be relied upon.

country market index, not to those Research Analysts’ coverage universe. If it does not appear in the Important Disclosures section of this report, the certifying Research

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Nan Juen released its 2Q26 results on 12 August. We provide 
our views in this report.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat on non-op gain. Nan Juen’s 2Q26 
earnings came in at TWD219m (EPS: TWD3.1), which was 5% higher than 
our estimate but 6% lower than consensus. Revenue was slightly below 
expectations, while gross margin edged up 0.2pp QoQ to 41.8%, which 
was modestly below both our and the street's estimates, mainly due to 
lower-than-expected revenue contribution from high-ASP regular server rail 
kits. Operating margin similarly rose 0.2pp QoQ to 29.7%, following the 
gross margin trend. Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected non-
operating gain of c.TWD21m, mainly driven by FX gains and government 
subsidies.

Business outlook. Looking into 3Q26, we expect shipments of one of its 
CSP client's regular server rail kits to remain weak, with 2Q26 monthly 
shipment declining to below 30k sets per month (vs. over 80k sets per 
month in 1Q26), while shipments to other CSP client should remain stable. 
As a result, we lower our 2026E regular server revenue forecast to 
TWD2.0bn from TWD2.4bn previously. That said, with Nan Juen now in 
mass production for both its GPU and ASIC projects, we expect AI server 
rail kit shipments to drive further revenue growth in 3Q26 and also lift 
margins, consistent with our previous expectations. Nan Juen's own Vera 
Rubin shipment schedule remains on track, targeting late-3Q26 shipments 
to its primary CSP customer. On ASIC, the mass production status by the 
CSP further solidifies our conviction. We maintain our 20-30% supply share 
assumption for this generation of ASIC in 2H26E and 2027E. Combined 
with the structural margin improvement from Vietnam, a regular server 
rebound could push gross margins above our current estimates, reinforcing 
our positive view on the stock. We remain positive on Nan Juen's product 
mix shift toward higher-margin server rail kits and its expanding AI server 
contribution. Overall, we believe its EPS growth trajectory over 2026-28E 
remains firmly on track.

What we recommend: We cut our 2026-27E EPS by 11-13% to reflect the 
current weakness in its regular server rail kit orders, but raise our 2028E 
EPS by c.2% to factor in the latest positive update on ASIC and AI margin 
contribution. We reaffirm Buy (1) and 12M TP of TWD1,022, based on a 
PER of 36x (vs. its past-3-year range of 11-90x), applied to our 4-quarter-
forward EPS forecast. Key downside risk: worse-than-expected AI server 
demand.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 3-8% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely as we are more positive on Nan Juen’s gross margin.

19 August 2026

Company profile 
Founded in 1984, Nan Juen International Co., LTD. is a Taiwan-based slide and rail kit manufacturer 
headquartered in Taoyuan City. The company began its operations as a producer of furniture and 
kitchen rail kits, leveraging its mechanical engineering expertise to gradually expand its product 
portfolio. Building on decades of slide mechanism design experience, Nan Juen extended its capabilities 
into the server rail kit market, which has since become the primary growth driver of the business.

Nan Juen's current product portfolio spans two core categories: server rail kits and others (including 
kitchen rail kits, office furniture rail kits, etc.). Within the server rail kit segment, the company produces 
both regular server rail kits and AI server rail kits, serving major CSPs globally. The company has been 
listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange under the ticker 6584 TT.

### Page 5

5

Nan Juen International (6584 TT): 19 August 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 19 Aug 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Juen’sboard consists of 9 directors, including 4 independent directors. The total number of
directors is slightly lower than that of other listed companies in our coverage. Nan Juen held 6
board meetings in 2025; the actual attendee rate was 98%.
Most of the board members are Nan Juen’sfounder's family members, which we believe
presents downside risks to independent governance. However, the independent directors have
diversified backgrounds with expertise in accounting and management, a positive for the board's
quality.
Capital management 2
Nan Juen declared a DPS of TWD2.5 in 2025, representing a payout ratio of 48%. The payout
ratio was in line with the level of 45-60% during 2021-24. As a rail kit manufacturer, Nan Juen is
set to deliver solid revenue growth and FCF over our forecast horizon amidst strong ASP
increase in the server rail kit market.
Related party &
transaction 1 The company did not report any related-party transactions in 2025. We see limited risks from
related-party transactions.
S Product quality &
safety 2
Nan Juen has implemented a Project Management Information System (PMIS) to streamline
R&D workflows and enhance cross-departmental collaboration. The company is further planning
to introduce a Product Lifecycle Management (PLM) system, integrating the full product lifecycle
from concept through to manufacturing and after-sales service. Beyond operational efficiency,
the PLM adoption reflects Nan Juen's commitment to responsible product stewardship, enabling
better oversight of material consumption and environmental impact across its operations. The
company continues to advance its green product programme.
S Product design &
lifecycle management 2
Nan Juen is actively reducing its greenhouse gas emissions under a core commitment to net-
zero carbon operations, aligned with Taiwan's 2050 net-zero emission policy. The company has
adopted ISO 14064-1 GHG inventory standards and maintains ISO 14001:2015 environmental
management system certification, providing a structured framework for monitoring and improving
energy use and emissions across its operations. Practical measures include regular audits of
high-energy-consuming equipment, employee sustainability training, and collaboration with
supply chain partners on green procurement. Progress is tracked through internal audits and
external verification, with findings reviewed periodically to refine the company's decarbonisation
strategy.

"1": the security could outperform the local index by more than 15% over the next 12 months. 
"2": the security is expected to outperform the local index by 5-15% over the next 12 months.

"4": the security is expected to underperform the local index by 5-15% over the next 12 months. 
"5": the security could underperform the local index by more than 15% over the next 12 months.

•ASIC Roadmap Intact Despite Broader TPU Multi-sourcing. Google 
appears to be broadening  its TPU ecosystem as recent news (Yahoo 
Finance, 18th August 2026) suggests they are accelerating diversification of 
their custom-silicon ecosystem, with AMD potentially taking a significantly 
bigger role (Seeking Alpha, 18 August 2026). Marvell has formally 
announced a broad custom-chip agreement with Google covering AI 
processors, storage and networking technologies, with potential revenue of 
up to US$120bn through FY33 if associated targets are achieved. In our 
view, neither development indicates that existing MediaTek programs have 
been either cancelled or transferred. Rather, we believe the announcements 
appear consistent with Google building a broader multi-vendor ecosystem 
of its own as TPU deployments expand rapidly.
•Limited Impact on MediaTek’s Confirmed 2027–28 product pipeline. We 
see limited implications for MediaTek’s near-term AI ASIC outlook because 
its next two years are increasingly defined by projects already under 
development rather than future RFQs. MediaTek reiterated in its 2Q26 
earnings call that its first AI accelerator ASIC will enter production in 4Q26, 
with data-center revenue exceeding US$2bn in 2026 and scaling 
substantially increasing in 2027. More importantly, the second ASIC is 
already progressing through advanced packaging qualification, with yield 
and reliability on track for high-volume production in 2028.  
•More than accelerators in AI semiconductor.  To reinforce learning and 
agentic AI requirement, we see a rising demand for  more general-purpose 
computing alongside tensor acceleration. Google increasingly appears to 
be optimizing different silicon architectures for training, inference, RL and 
other workloads rather than converging toward a single TPU 
implementation. Based on Marvell's announcement, this covers a broad 
range of custom silicon—including AI processors, storage and

•Google is scaling rapidly enough to support multiple silicon partners. MediaTek itself raised its 2027 accelerator

our view, MediaTek’s long experience managing very large consumer-semiconductor supply chains will therefore

become even more meaningful when AI ASIC volumes scale.

Mediatek 
Valuation 
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year 
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also 
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should 
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication. 
Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its 
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT 
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity. 
Risks 
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from 
AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic 
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries. 
Advanced Micro Devices 
(AMD.O; US$466.42; 1; 19 Aug 26; 16:00) 
Valuation 
Our TP on AMD is $575. We value AMD using SOTP analysis with data center GPU/CPU biz at $267/$207 (27/25x 2028 PE), 
client $31 (15x), gaming $2 (6x), embedded $29 (20x), and net cash per share ~$40. Our target PE multiples are in-line with 
respective peer group averages.

Risks 
Competition: AMD competes directly with Intel in the microprocessor market. Consequently, any fluctuations in market share 
between AMD and Intel could result in risk to our estimates. AMD competes directly with NVIDIA in the graphics and AI GPU 
market. Consequently, any fluctuations in market share between AMD and NVIDIA could result in risk to our estimates.
PC End Market: AMD derives roughly 30% of sales from the PC industry, which is highly dependent on IT spending. Therefore, 
any major uptick/downtick in IT spending could result in upside/downside to our estimates and rating for AMD.
Valuation: While AMD is currently trading slightly above its historical range, revisions to consensus revenue estimates could 
result in high volatility to AMD stock.
Tender Offer: Any public or private tender offer for all or part of AMD’s business could drive the stock higher and cause us to re-
assess our rating.
If the impact on the company from any of these factors proves to be greater than we anticipate, the stock will likely have 
difficulty achieving our target price. However, should they be less than anticipated, the stock could trade above our target 
price. 
Alphabet Inc 
(GOOGL.O; US$344.72; 1; 19 Aug 26; 16:00) 
Valuation 
Our $447 target price is based on ~28x our GAAP EPS of $15.72 in 2027E. While a 28x multiple is a premium to the market and 
to Google’s historical multiple range, given Google Cloud revenue growth is reaccelerating on TPU and Gemini demand as 
query volume strength continues, we believe the shares warrant a premium to the market and to Alphabet’s historical multiple. 
We reiterate our Buy rating on shares given our view that Google's product halo could lead to continued Search usage, and we 
are impressed with Gemini 3.5 Flash and Google’s newer AI models.

Risks to Alphabet achieving our target price include: 1) adverse economic and/or consumer spending trends could hinder

Apple, Inc.
(AAPL.O; US$316.83; 1; 19 Aug 26; 16:00)
Valuation
Our target price for Apple is $365. We value AAPL at $365 or 33x P/E on our FY2028 EPS estimate. Our 33x P/E is about a 20%
premium to Apple’s historical level. We believe a premium is warranted to reflect expanding gross margins (ex tariffs), growing
services sales mix, gradual Apple Intelligence adoption, and a strong balance sheet.
Risks
Key risks to our investment thesis and to Apple achieving our target price include the following:

1) Weaker macroeconomic conditions or shifting consumer demand could cause greater-than-expected deceleration or
contraction in the handset and smartphone markets. This would negatively impact Apple's prospects for growth, and the
shares may fail to achieve our target price as a result.
2) Uncertainty regarding US/China tensions could impact Apple's supply chain, as the company is heavily reliant on
suppliers/manufacturers in Taiwan and mainland China.

3) Regulatory risk remains as a major headwind including Digital Markets Act in Europe, which would push Apple to allow
alternative app store on its iPhones and iPads and reduce app store revenues in the region.
4) Heavyweight in the market index makes institutional investors cautious on adding more Apple positions.
Marvell Technology Inc
(MRVL.O; US$237.27; 1; 19 Aug 26; 16:00)
Valuation
Our $275 target price is based on a 35x P/E on CY27E EPS. The 35x P/E valuation is at a ~30% premium to the   current average

P/E of AI semis peers. The premium primarily reflects the company’s M&A target factor.
Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include the following: 1) storage weakness; 2) networking growth does not
materialize; and 3) share losses in both storage and networking.
Upside risks to our target price include: 1) higher acquisition synergies; and 2) faster share gains in storage and networking.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-21
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: After market hours, Bizlink released its 2Q26 results and held 
an investor call today (21 August). Its gross margin in 2Q26 improved QoQ 
to 30.8% from 28.8% due to ongoing product mix optimisation and rising 
capacity utilisation. We believe Bizlink will be one of the key beneficiaries 
from AI infrastructure (for HPC and semi cap), and rising product 
complexity should underpin its margin expansion in the following years. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call. We now forecast an earnings CAGR of 48.2% 
from 45.6% previously over 2026-28E.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat slightly. Bizlink’s 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD23,281m (+11.6% QoQ, +37.5% YoY) was in line with our estimate 
and the consensus estimate. Its gross margin was 30.7% (vs. 28.8% in 
1Q26 and 31.1% in 2Q25), higher than our estimate (30.2%) but in line with 
the consensus estimate (30.7%). The recovery in gross margin vs. 1Q26 is 
due to product mix improvement and a higher utilisation rate. Its operating 
profit of TWD4,010m (+29.0% QoQ, +39.9% YoY) slightly beat our and the 
consensus estimates by 2.5% and 1.1%, respectively. Thus, its operating 
margin of 17.2% (vs. 14.9% in 1Q26 and 16.9% in 2Q25) was roughly in 
line with our estimate (17.0%) and the consensus (17.3%). Overall, its 
2Q26 net profit of TWD2,982m (+31.1% QoQ, +47.3% YoY) also slightly 
beat our and the Bloomberg estimates by 4.8% and 2.9%, respectively. Its 
2Q26 basic EPS finished at TWD15.28.

Key highlights from results call. During the call, Bizlink mentioned that 
its copper, power and optical solutions should lead it to win more layers of 
increasingly integrated AI infrastructure, and the product complexity (higher 
power density, bandwidth and integration requirement) trend is also positive 
for its gross margin expansion. The acquisition of Interplex Datacom (see 
memo) is expected to close in 2H26 and should strengthen Bizlink’s 
capabilities in precision metal fabrication and mechanical and structural 
components, as well as electrical and connectivity capabilities. We raised 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-5%, mainly on our more aggressive revenue 
assumptions on its order share win potential.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD3,065 (from TWD3,053), based on an unchanged PER of 30x 
(equal to a 0.62x PEG over 2026-28E; at the high-end of its past-3-year 
range of 6-34x) applied to our 1-year forward diluted EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: weaker-than-expected orders from secular growth drivers 
(HPC and semi cap) and slower-than-expected gross-margin expansion.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-5% above the consensus, as our 
more aggressive revenue and operating margin assumptions.

21 August 2026

2Q26 results: better than expected

➢ 2Q26 net profit slightly beat our and the Bloomberg consensus by 3-5% 
➢ Benefitting from spec upgrade on its copper, power and optic solutions 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD3,065

Company profile 
Founded in 1996 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2011, Bizlink Holding (Bizlink) is a vertically-
integrated provider for interconnect solutions for IT & CE (28% of 2025 revenue), electrical appliances 
(16%), automotive (15%) and industrial (39%). Major clients include Dell, HP, Tesla, Polaris, Thermo 
King, Dyson and GE Healthcare.

### Page 5

5

Bizlink Holding (3665 TT): 21 August 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 31 Mar 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Bizlink are not the same individual. Bizlink has 7 directors,
including 3 independent directors, on its board. With backgrounds in engineering (directors) and
materials science/law (independent directors), we view this as positive as we believe that their
wide-ranging experience will help lead Bizlink under the competitive environment.
Capital management 2
Bizlink's payout ratio has gradually declined over the past decade, from 79% in 2016 to 33% in
2025. Considering the company’smergers over this period, we view the drop in payout ratio as
reasonable.
Related party &
transaction 2 No revenue and COGS are generated from related-party transactions – we see no issues.
S Supply chain
management 1
To improve its product quality, Bizlink has high-standard/eco-friendly production processes, and
places high importance on client feedback. The company received an overall positive rating of
93% in its 2023 customer survey. Bizlink has a 100% local sourcing policy (achieved 75% in
2023) – this is to ensure a high standard of services from suppliers and to help reduce its carbon
footprint. Furthermore, Bizlink has more than 500 suppliers globally; only 25% of its procurement
is from the company’stop-10 suppliers. Though we believe Bizlink is relatively safe from material
sourcing risks in the long term, the company has still been impacted by the recent plastic and
copper price hikes.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

BizLink

2Q26 results in line; positive on AI & semi 
equipment; trim PO to NT$3,088; Buy

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,088 TWD | Price: 2,275 TWD

2Q in line; GM reflects deployment cycle and product mix 
BizLink’s 2Q GM/OPM of 30.7%/17.2% came in roughly in line with BofAe of 30.9%/ 
17.4% and consensus of 30.7%/17.3%, with 3%/1% higher sales than BofAe/consensus. 
We believe 2Q GM/OPM mainly reflects different deployment cycle among HPC clients 
and product-mix change. HPC comprised 42% of 2Q sales (with a higher mix from power 
vs. data), vs. 45% in 1Q (evenly sales split between power and data). Industrial remained 
the second-largest segment at 35% in 2Q, vs. 34% in 1Q. Yet, sales mix from lighter-
margin EA business rose to 12% in 2Q, vs. 8% in 1Q, after consolidating newly acquired 
PAS. With opex control and a lighter tax rate, NP was 4%/3% above BofAe/consensus. 
Positive on AI & semi equip.; BofAe 2H QoQ sales growth 
Mgmt. remains positive on AI and semi equipment, and sees them as two key structural 
growth drivers. For AI, mgmt. highlighted rising power density and data speed with more 
integrated system requirements. For semi equipment, mgmt. cited a higher value-add 
and clear order visibility into 2H27 as the higher-level assembler and system integrator 
from purely component maker. We now expect 3Q/4Q26 sales to grow 18%/10% QoQ 
and the GM to improve to 31.8%/32.8% (vs. 28.8%/30.7% in 1Q/2Q), supported by new 
AI GPU/ASIC platform launches and sustainable demand from semi equipment business.  
Lower PO to NT$3,088 but reiterate Buy 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 2-4% to reflect in-line 2Q26 results, solid AI momentum, sales 
from Interplex Datacom acquisition from 2027 (see our 11 June note), and subtotal 5% 
share dilution from new GDR and ECB issued in mid-Aug. We trim PO to NT$3,088 (28x 
2027E EPS) from NT$3,230 (30x 2027E EPS), with the valuation base unchanged but a 
lower P/E multiple. We now use 28x (3SD above average) on potentially slower margin 
expansion amid clients’ different deployment schedule. Yet, we reiterate Buy as BizLink 
benefits from AI copper/power/optical cable demand with volume/content increase.

Company Sector 
Connectors, Passive Components & Distribution

Company Description 
BizLink is a global interconnect solutions provider, making 
products like cables, wire harnesses connectors, etc. 
BizLink's key business segments are IT DataCom (43% of 
2025 sales), Industrial (34%), Automotive (11%), and 
Electrics Appliance (11%). BizLink is headquartered in the 
Silicon Valley, California, US. Globally, it has 40 production 
sites post INBG acquisition in 2022. It was founded in 1996 
and was listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2011.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to BizLink in view of (1) 
cable/connector volume/content increase under strong AI 
server market demand and rising computing power, (2) 
BizLink's solid delivery in better-margin HPC business, (3) 
solid partnership with Credo for AEC business and 
expansion into optical business, (4) industrial business 
bottoming out, (5) ongoing margin expansion with 
improving mix.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
BizLink (BIZLF) 
Our PO of NT$3,088 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. Our valuation multiple of 28x (3SD 
above historical average since IPO) reflects positive outlook for better-margin HPC 
business to ride on multi-year AI demand, content/volume growth driven by ongoing 
copper/power/optical cable/connector development for AI, industrial business overall 
bottoming out, robust semi equipment demand with over one year order visibility, and 
expanding margins.

Upside risks: (1) stronger demand in AI, (2) stronger semi-cap/datacenter market 
demand, (3) improving profitability for EV business, (4) margin upside from more stable 
raw material prices (copper, plastic, gold) and operating leverage, (5) favorable FX 
movement, and (6) more synergies from M&A.

Downside risks: (1) delayed order from AI business, (2) intensifying competition and/or 
pricing pressure, (3) weaker macro to hinder Industrial market demand, (4) more intense 
competition in EV business, (4) GM pressure from higher raw material prices and/or 
increasing labor cost in China/US/Europe/Malaysia, (5) unfavorable FX movement, and 
(6) less synergies from M&A.

What’s Changed
Global Unichip Corp
(3443.TW) From To

Alchip Technologies Ltd
(3661.TW) From To
Price Target NT$5,088.00 NT$5,888.00
With the complexity of TPU v10, we see room for multiple
design service providers to contribute 'blocks' for MediaTek to
build v10 chips. We reiterate  OW on MediaTek, and  raise  GUC

and Alchip PTs on expected larger revenue size in 2027/2028.
What’s new? Investors have recently been interested in several chip designers who
stated they are associated with Google TPU-related projects. In Joe Moore's report
Marvell: Thoughts on MRVL Google Opportunity (19 Aug 2026), there is some
discussion that AMD may also be involved in TPUv10. On July 31, Taiwan's GUC
stated its confidence in engaging with  project design services for Google TPU, while

on Aug 14, Taiwan's Alchip also indicated Google’s COT (customer-owned tooling)
business model would provide Alchip with an opportunity to win Google-related
design services. So, is everyone winning?
We think MediaTek remains the major coordinator for TPU v10:   According to Joe,
Marvell is winning additional content around TPU rather than replacing core TPU
itself. We would not yet assume it is displacing the existing TPU designers
(Broadcom in the US [covered by Joe], and MediaTek in Taiwan). Our view from Asia

is that we do see more 'blocks to build' for TPU v10 so more design service providers
may be included in the same project. We see three key trends in Google TPU v10:
1.  KGD (known good die) for compute die, which means Google owns the
compute die design and the tool (photomask), and has some flexibility to
include other design services for that compute die backend-design service.
2. Adoption of 3.5D packaging. It requires 3D IC stack the compute die on other

attached dies, and that needs a 3D IC IP vendor for the design service.
3.  Larger reticle size. The chip reticle size may further expand to >12 reticles,
vs. currently around 9 reticles.
We think TPU v10 continues to use Intel EMIB-T given the larger reticle size, while
MediaTek’s SerDes IP is also improving, creating some stickiness to MediaTek’s
design service. We suggest to accumulate MediaTek on its recent share price dip.
The stock trades at 12x 2028 EPS.

Raising target prices for both GUC and Alchip -  attractive on 2028 valuations
• Raising GUC PT to NT$6,288, given better TSMC 3nm allocation in 2027, and
its Meta/Microsoft projects wins. The company also announced the issue of a
CB to enlarge business scale. Stock trades at 25x 2028 EPS.
• Raising Alchip PT to NT5,888, based on US$8bn revenue contribution from
Trainium4,  implying 60% Y/Y of 2028 EPS. Stock trades at 10x 2028 EPS.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

Idea
2
1. Google AI ASIC  TPU provided by MediaTek in Asia
TPU v10 is the project that can involve many
We see three key trends in Google TPU v10: 1) KGD (known good die) for compute die,
which means Google owns the compute die design and the tool (photomask), and has
some flexibility to include other design services for that compute die backend-design
service. 2) Adoption of 3.5D packaging. It requires 3D IC stack the compute die on other
attached dies, and that needs a 3D IC IP vendor for that part of the design service. 3)

Larger reticle size. The chip reticle size may further expand to >12 reticles, vs. currently
around 9 reticles.
Investors have recently been interested in several chip designers who stated they are
associated with Google TPU-related projects, including but not limited to:
1. 1) Marvell (covered by Joe Moore) which disclosed an expanded partnership with
Google (covered by Brian Nowak) across a range of custom silicon programs that
attach to the TPU ecosystem, including inference accelerators, storage controllers,

NICs, memory interface controllers, and near memory compute.
2. 2) In Joe's report Marvell: Thoughts on MRVL Google Opportunity (19 Aug 2026),
there is some discussion that AMD may also be involved in TPUv10.
3. 3) On July 31, Taiwan's GUC stated its confidence in engaging with  project design
services for Google TPU.
4. 4) On Aug 14, Taiwan's Alchip also indicated Google’s COT (customer-owned
tooling) business model would provide Alchip with an opportunity to win Google-

related design services. So, is everyone winning?
Our view from Asia is that we do see more 'blocks to build' for TPU v10 so more design
service providers may be included in the same project.

MediaTek TPU revenue could reach US$70bn by 2029 even

under a COT (customer-owned tooling) model
We believe MediaTek remains the major integrator of TPU v10 (IceFish) and its
responsibility is very similar to what it does for TPU v9 (HumuFish), in that: 1) I/O die
design with its own SerDes IP; 2) chip packaging integration compute die KGD, I/O die, and
HBM chips. MediaTek’s content per chip (ASP) can increase at TPU v10, given larger die
size of the I/O dies vs. TPU v9.
Our assumption for MediaTek's 2027 and 2028 TPU revenue remains unchanged at US$13-

15bn and US$$43-45bn, respectively, by forecasting 3mn units of TPU v8t in 2027 and
1mn/3mn units of TPUv8t/v9 in 2028. For 2029, we expect the demand for TPU v9 will
continue to increase while TPU v10 starts to ramp up. We currently project 4mn units of
TPU v9 in 2029 and around 1mn units  of TPU v10, leading to around US$70bn of TPU
revenue.
MediaTek TPU v10 single chip ASP/revenue recognition could still be higher than TPU
v9: On TPU v10, we believe the project will be closer to a COT model where different

parties might handle different functions or "blocks" within the projects. For example,
Google will handle the design and production of compute die and provide the die to
MediaTek as a KGD (known good die) and let MediaTek finish the final packaging at Intel
EMIB-T. Google owns the compute die design and the tool (photomask), and has some
flexibility to include other design services for that compute die backend-design service.
MediaTek's role in handling the I/O die could remain the same as what it has done for TPU

v8t and TPUv9. Even without recognizing the KGD/compute die revenue, our industry
checks suggest MediaTek could still book higher single chip ASP/revenue than TPU v9,
where we now expect the ASP for MediaTek could be US$18k per TPU v10.

Google CPU should be the largest customer for GUC in 2026-2028: In 2026, Google
CPU volume is falling a little short of our original forecast given the capacity constraints.
We therefore now forecast only 1.2-1.3mn units of Google Axion2 CPU in 2026 vs. our
initial forecast of 1.5mn-2mn. By assuming chip ASP at around US$700, we now see 2026
Google CPU contributing US$900-1000mn.
For 2027, we have been seeing strong upward demand revisions for Google CPU, including

some push out of Axion2 from 2026 and the ramp of next generation Axion3. We now
expect Google CPU revenue of close to US$2.8bn, including 3mn units of Axion2 and
500k units of Axion3. In addition, we also expect the chip ASP of Axion3 to double from
Axion2 given the increase of 3nm compute die from one to two.
For 2028, we now forecast only 2.5mn units of Axion3, lower than Axion2's annual run
rate of 3-4mn units, mostly also driven by the doubling of compute die.

CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin for Google CPU – likely around 10% –
because this is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design
support (e.g., silicon interposer and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for
the ASIC designs. We also expect Axion3 margin to be slightly lower than Axion2 given
higher chip ASP or larger project size.
Note, these forecasts are from Asia Pacific research and incorporate supply chain and

industry checks.

2. AWS AI ASIC  Trainium provided by  Alchip in Asia
Alchip might achieve US$8bn revenue for Trainium4 in 2028: During its latest earnings
call, Alchip management mentioned that it believes its Trainium4 TAM could be similar to
Google TPU. Our industry checks suggest the Trainium4 volume in 2028 could be at least
2.5mn units. By suggesting Trainium4 ASP of around US$10k without HBM and Alchip
getting around 30% of the turnkey allocation (similar to Trainium3 of around one-third),

we now see the revenue contribution of Trainium4 to be US$8bn in 2028, higher than our
initial forecast of US$4bn. We therefore also raise our price target for Alchip to NT$5,888,
implying >50% upside and a potential win of a second CSP customer.

potential CoWoS out by 2027 year-end or 1H28.

raise GUC PT to NT$6388, given more TSMC 3nm wafer allocation in 2027, and also GUC’s
potential 3D IC design service for Google TPU v10 in 2028. Larger 3nm wafer allocation

also leads stronger Google CPU contribution in our forecasts.

### Page 7

Morgan Stanley Research 7
2027 Global GPU/ASIC Total Power Consumption
Implied chip volume vs. electricity consumption (GW)
frequently discussed, suggesting power supply remains a
global bottleneck
We recently published Asia-Pacific Technology: Connecting Dots: HK investors' feedback on
Asian semis (9 Aug 2026). During our recent Hong Kong marketing trip, investors
questioned whether power infrastructure can support our projected 2027 CoWoS-implied

chip volume of approximately 19mn units. Assuming an average TDP of 2kW per GPU/
ASIC, the installed base would require roughly 38GW of power capacity.
Conversely, when SpaceX (covered by Adam Jonas)  indicated plans to expand its
computing capacity from 2GW by the end of this year to nearly 10GW under an ambitious
scenario  (see SpaceX: Strong Beat/Outlook vs. Higher Capex), investors also asked what
this would imply for TSMC's CoWoS capacity consumption. Notably, Adam models only

3GW of incremental computing demand, reaching 5GW by end-2027. As a rough
illustration, the additional 8GW implied by SpaceX's target would equate to
approximately 4mn Rubin GPUs, or roughly 20% of the AI accelerators that TSMC could
produce in 2027.

Morgan Stanley Research 13
Aligning Blackwell and Rubin Chip Shipments with HGX and Rack Shipments
We analyzed Blackwell chip shipments versus rack shipments in our  last  June AI tracker.
We believe TSMC's Blackwell chip output, based on CoWoS-L capacity, aligns more closely
with underlying demand as reflected in AI capex trends.
We now incorporate HGX systems (8 GPUs per server) into our chip consumption model
and assume that nine HGX servers are equivalent to one NVL72 rack. We forecast 5.4mn

Blackwell units in 2026, with chip supply sufficient to meet Grace Blackwell NVL72
demand by 2H26.
Our latest checks also suggest shipments of nearly 7mn Rubin and Rubin Ultra units in
2027, while total Rubin NVL72 rack shipments could reach 90k units in 2027 (Exhibit 22).
All Blackwell chip "inventory" has proven to be supply-chain buffer stock and should be
fully absorbed by 2026. As a result, we see little reason for concern over inventory levels.

We expect Rubin to follow a similar pattern. Rubin should begin ramping up in 3Q26, with
rack shipments starting in 4Q26.

GUC: Relative valuation
Our price target of NT$6,288 implies 45x our 2027 EPS estimate, lower than +1 SD from
GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market

will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term, and therefore we believe
a further re-rating would be justified with project wins likely to come. Despite slower
recovery of non-AI business, we continue to appreciate GUC’s growing exposure to cloud
AI semis (including AI accelerators, AI server CPU, and BMC chips).

Alchip: Valuation methodology
We raise our price target from NT$5,088 to NT$5,888: As well as reflecting  our new
2028 earnings forecast, we cut some of our key assumptions given the ongoing
competition from global peers and more newcomers to compete with AI ASIC projects,
including GUC, QCOM, AMD, etc.
We continue to derive our base case value, which is also our price target, from a residual
income model. Our cost of equity of 10.4% (beta of 1.4, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and

risk-free rate of 2.0%) remains unchanged, while intermediate growth rate of  is cut from
16% to 12.5%, and terminal growth rate from  5.0% to 4%.
Our bear case value of NT$2,465 and bull case value of NT$6,880 are also raised from NT
$2,130 and NT$5,950, respectively.

Base case value, derived from a residual income model. We assume the cost of equity is
constant at 10.4% (2% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.4 beta). Our intermediate growth

MS Rating

▪ We continue to believe that AI ASICs will
outgrow AI GPUs in the coming years, and
Alchip is one of the few ASIC design service
providers that can deal with increasingly
complex designs (at 2nm) with good
production/design track records for large
leading edge chips.
▪ AWS's announcement of its 3nm
Trainium3 chip matches the timing of
Alchip's 3nm project win. The growing
number of use cases for Trainium (e.g.,
Apple's adoption) bodes well for Alchip's

revenue growth in 2026.
▪ We believe Alchip has strong potential to
win Trainium4.
▪ Our price target implies 26x our 2027e
EPS, which we ﬁnd undemanding vs. our
>100% revenue CAGR for 2025-28e.

market, amid continued proliferation of

NT$5,888.00
26x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and HPC projects start to
proliferate amid strong end demand,
resulting in steady growth in tape-outs and
volume. There might be a slowdown in
Alchip's Chinese customers' design activity
for leading-edge chips as a result of US
export controls; we believe this would be
largely offset by ASIC demand strength
from non-China regions.

end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs
and mass production. Alchip's leading-edge
project pipeline deteriorates, or some
customers turn directly to TSMC. AI GPU's
dominance in AI computing compresses the
market share gain potential for overall ASIC,
with customers starting to slow their
custom silicon developments - and therefore
ASIC procurement plans.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

評價處於歷史區間下緣，給予華航投資建議為 Buy ，目標價 26
元。  
燃油價格變動對於公司獲利影響數分析 : 公司 1H26 營運成本
結構中，燃油成本 38% 、人事 15% 、修護 13% 、折舊 12% 、場
站 10%、旅客服務 5%及其他 7%。研究部評估若以全年 1,650 萬
桶用油量、以及匯率 32 元 (美元 /台幣 )的假設基礎計算，在不考
慮燃油附加費的情況下，燃油價格每變動 10 美元，對於公司全
年 EPS 影響約為 0.87 元。   
預估華航 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.35 元 : 華航 1Q26 營收 569.97 億
元、YoY+8.01% ，稅後獲利 51.69 億元， YoY+23.51% ，稅後 EPS 
0.84 元。受惠於春節需求旺季， 1Q26 客運營收 YoY+9.1% ；貨
運方面，受惠 AI 設備需求強勁，加上中東戰爭推升貨運單位收
益， 1Q26 貨運營收 YoY+5.2% 。 2Q26 貨運受惠於 AI 相關需求
持續暢旺，貨運量 QoQ+8% ，加上反映燃油附加費的調漲，貨
運單位收益 QoQ+33.2% ；客運方面，因進入需求淡季，加上客
運的燃油附加費無法及時轉嫁， 2Q26 華航客運量 QoQ-2.5% 、
客運價 QoQ-0.1% 。受到燃油價格上漲的衝擊，華航 2Q26 毛利
率由 1Q26 的 20.6% 下滑至 11.12% ，稅後獲利 14.13 億元， YoY-
63.36% ，稅後 EPS 0.23 元。 3Q26 進入客運旺季，貨運需求成長
態勢持續，皆有利支撐單位收益的表現，加上航空燃油成本壓
力較 2Q26 減緩，預估單季稅後獲利 44.42 億元， YoY +50.28% ，
稅後 EPS 0.72 元。整體而言，預估華航 2026 年稅後獲利 144.52
億元， YoY-1.69% ，稅後 EPS 2.35 元。展望 2027 年，假設政治
地緣因素造成的原油的風險溢價有望趨緩、 AI 及半導體等設備
需求維持成長，預估華航 2027 年稅後獲利 153.54 億元，稅後
EPS 2.5 元。

2 群益投顧 
  公司 ESG 政策 ：  
(一 ) 溫室氣體減量策略及具體行動計畫  
為符合國際減碳管理趨勢，參考科學基礎減量目標 (SBT) 思維，合併公司為台灣
首家運輸業者同時訂定飛航與地面溫室氣體自願減量目標，訂定永續航空燃油
(SAF) 短中長程使用目標。 航空業用油燃燒排放之溫室氣體佔總排放量九成以上 ，
因此除持續推動機隊更新外，合併公司亦透過專責「航機用油管理小組」持續強
化用油效率精進對策，擬定多項具體執行措施，從規劃面、管理面、執行面全面
提升整體用油績效。  
(二 ) 溫室氣體減量目標達成情形  
地面作業持續實施 ISO 50001 能源管理系統，考量節能減碳效益並納入決策因
子，完善監督量測機制、提升能源績效管理細緻度，整合利害相關方關切事項，
打造低碳營運及辦公環境。。  
(三 ) 氣候風險之辨識、評估及管理流程如何整合於整體風險管理制度   
華航根據氣候變遷對企業整體營運的衝擊為基礎，參考 TCFD 指引及日本環境省
發佈的「氣候關聯風險．機會情境導入分析指引  ver2.0 」，鑑別出內部作業的關
鍵單元與作業設施，如運輸服務過程所需要的能資源、航空器、空調設施及其他
用電、用油需求 … 等，進行相關之影響與衝擊程度分析，並建立改善策略與執行
結果之追蹤、管考與持續改善機制。  
(四 ) 提升能源使用效率政策  
1. 航空器能源效率提升  
(1) 提高航機用油效率、減少油耗量與降低碳排放。  
(2) 執行機隊更新、引進節油技術 /永續航空燃油。  
(3) 提升載客 /貨率、優化航線與航管作業。  
2. 地面作業能源管制  
(1) 提升辦公室及地面作業能源效率。  
(2) 設定減量目標及建立年度改善方案。  
(3) 適時擴大再生能源使用及增加再生能源裝置容量。。

3 群益投顧 
IATA 預估 2026 年亞太地區航空客運量 YoY+5.1% ：  
根據 IATA 統計， 2024 年及 2025 年全球航空客運量 YoY 分別為 10.6% 及 5.3% ，
2026 年受到美伊戰爭影響，預估全球航空客運量 YoY 下滑至 2.1% ，主因在於中
東地區客運量呈現衰退所致，而亞太地區 2026 年客運量 YoY 則預估為 5.1% ( 圖
一 )。  
圖一、全球航空客運量

資料來源： IATA ，群益投顧彙整  
全球貨運量增速放緩：  
貨運方面 ， 由於中東區域為航空貨運樞紐之一 ， 中東戰事導致航空貨運運力受限 ，
進而推升運價上漲。貨量方面，根據 IATA 預估，在 2025 年的高基期下， 2026
年全球航空貨運量 YoY 將放緩至 0.7%( 圖二 )，其中亞太地區仍是主要增長動能
來源， 2026 年貨量需求 YoY 有望達 5.6% 。

4 群益投顧 
公司機隊規劃：  
華航 2Q26 季底客機機隊數量為 69 架，後續將持續引進 A321neo 加入區域航線
運能，透過 A350-900 及波音 787-9 客機優化長程線的運力結構，同時汰除 A330-
300 及波音 737-800 客機， 2026 年底整體客機機隊規模預計達 74 架。  
貨機方面，目前貨機機隊共 18 架，未來將逐漸汰換 744F 機型、擴大 777F 機隊，
並引進次世代貨機 777-8F 機型。  
燃油價格波動對於公司獲利影響數分析 ：  
公司 1H26 營運成本結構中，燃油成本 38% 、人事 15% 、修護 13% 、折舊 12% 、
場站 10%、旅客服務 5%及其他 7%。研究部評估若以全年 1,650 萬桶用油量、以
及匯率 32 元 (美元 /台幣 )的假設基礎計算，則在不考慮燃油附加費的情況下，燃
油價格每變動 10 美元，對於公司全年 EPS 影響約為 0.87 元 (表一 )。  
表一、燃油價格變動對於華航獲利影響數分析  
資料來源：群益投顧預估彙整  
預估華航 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.35 元：  
華航 2025 年稅後獲利 147 億元， YoY+2.2% ，稅後 EPS 2.39 元。  
華航 1Q26 營收 569.97 億元、 YoY+8.01% ，稅後獲利 51.69 億元， YoY+23.51% ，
稅後 EPS 0.84 元。受惠於春節需求旺季， 1Q26 客運營收 YoY+9.1% ；貨運方面，
受惠 AI 設備需求強勁，加上中東戰爭推升貨運單位收益， 1Q26 貨運營收
YoY+5.2% 。  
2Q26 貨運受惠於 AI 相關需求持續暢旺，貨運量 QoQ+8% ，加上反映燃油附加費
的調漲，貨運單位收益 QoQ+33.2% ；客運方面，因進入需求淡季，加上客運的燃
油附加費無法及時轉嫁， 2Q26 華航客運量 QoQ-2.5% 、客運價 QoQ-0.1% 。受到
燃油價格上漲的衝擊，華航 2Q26 毛利率由 1Q26 的 20.6% 下滑至 11.12% ，稅後
獲利 14.13 億元， YoY-63.36% ，稅後 EPS 0.23 元。  
3Q26 進入客運旺季，貨運需求成長態勢持續，皆有利支撐單位收益的表現，加
上航空燃油成本壓力較 2Q26 減緩 ， 預估單季稅後獲利44.42 億元 ，YoY +50.28% ，
稅後 EPS 0.72 元。  
整體而言，預估華航 2026 年稅後獲利 144.52 億元， YoY-1.69% ，稅後 EPS 2.35
元。  
展望 2027 年，假設政治地緣因素造成的原油的風險溢價有望趨緩、 AI 及半導體
等設備需求維持成長，預估華航 2027 年稅後獲利 153.54 億元，稅後 EPS 2.5 元。
投資建議：  
考量獲利低點已過， 2H26 營運重拾成長動能，目前評價處於歷史區間下緣，給
予華航投資建議為 Buy ，目標價 26 元。

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

神基 (3005 TT)  Buy

營收低谷已過，且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議  
投資建議 ： 考量公司營收低谷已過 ，2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26，
且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 營收 YOY +17.37% ，成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件：
2Q26 神基營收為 115.13 億元， QOQ +23.68% ， YOY +17.37% ，
營收成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件 ， 營收 YOY 分別為 +27%
和 +40% ，其中電子件裡面，以強固型電腦表現較為強勁，漢通
則有部分受到筆電需求下滑的影響 。 機構件營收則為 YOY -1% ，
主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響，不過營收 QOQ 為 +9%，顯示
需求有回溫跡象。毛利率較 1Q26 增加，雖有受到原物料漲價而
影響毛利率，不過因為較高毛利的強固型電腦拉貨強勁，使得
整體毛利率逆勢成長。  
2H26 營收將呈現旺季水準，其中強固型電腦產品為主要成長動
能： 公司給予營收展望為 1H26:2H26=48~50:50~52 ， 2026 年營
收 YOY 為 5~10% ，並表示有望達高標或是優於展望。主要成長
動能為強固型電腦產品，由全球軍事預算提升所帶動，除了軍
事外，公用事業和大眾運輸也有專案在進行，故公司認為 2026
年強固型電腦營收 YOY 為 20~30% ， 有機會高於30%。漢 通2026
年營收 YOY 為 10~16% ， 主要是強固型電腦的強勁成長所帶動 ，
不過受到筆電需求下滑的影響，整體成長有受到拖累。綜合機
構件方面， 2026 年營收 YOY 為 -8~-12% ，主要是受到筆電需求
下滑的影響，不過有看到 2H26 筆電需求有較 1H26 回溫。汽車
機構件 2026 年營收為 YOY 接近 10% 的成長。毛利率方面，雖
然有受到記憶體漲價的影響，不過主要成長動能的強固型電腦
產品毛利率高於整體平均，一正一反下，預估 2026 年毛利率與
2025 年相當。綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 436.09 億元， YOY 
+10.55% ；稅後純益為 54.41 億元， YOY +4.10% ；稅後 EPS 8.72
元。預估 2027 年營收為 473.87 億元， YOY +8.66% ；稅後純益
為 60.50 億元， YOY +11.18% ；稅後 EPS 9.69 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：

公司簡介：  
神基 (3005 TT) 成立於 1989 年，為聯華神通集團旗下的神達電腦 (3706 TT) 與
美國奇異航太部門（ GE Aerospace ）共同合資成立，為全球第二大國際攜帶
型強固型電腦品牌商，產品應用領域涵蓋軍方、警方、公共事業、通訊、製
造、以及交通運輸等。旗下的漢通科技為國際專業光機電解決方案的供應
商，主要產品包含電源暨新能源解決方案、工業顯示器解決方案、模具原型
打樣解決方案等等。機構件的部分主要分為兩大領域：綜合機構件（筆電與
遊戲機）與汽車機構件，神基擁有領先的 MHCM 技術（ Multi-medium Heat 
& Cool Molding ，多介質急冷急熱成型技術）主要用於強固型電腦及電子機
構件；汽車機構件的部分，則主要生產輕金屬壓鑄製程的汽車內構件，其中
安全帶轉軸為全球市佔率第一。神基另有投資豐達科 (3004 TT) ，持股
36.77% ，有編入公司的合併報表，為航太發動機扣件的製造商。營收比重方
面， 2Q26 電子件佔 54% ，機構件佔 34% ，航太扣件佔 12% 。

2Q26 營收 YOY +17.37% ，成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件：  
2Q26 神基營收為 115.13 億元， QOQ +23.68% ， YOY +17.37% ；毛利率為
31.69% ，較 1Q26 增加 0.63 個百分點 ； 稅後純益為15.73 億元 ，QOQ +44.65% ，
YOY +12.19% ；稅後 EPS 2.52 元。營收成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件，
營收 YOY 分別為 +27% 和 +40% ，其中電子件裡面，以強固型電腦表現較為
強勁，漢通則有部分受到筆電需求下滑的影響。機構件營收則為 YOY -1% ，
主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響，不過營收 QOQ 為 +9% ，顯示需求有回溫
跡象。毛利率較 1Q26 增加，雖有受到原物料漲價而影響毛利率，不過因為
較高毛利的強固型電腦拉貨強勁，使得整體毛利率逆勢成長。  
2H26 營收將呈現旺季水準，其中強固型電腦產品為主要成長動能：  
公司給予營收展望為 1H26:2H26=48~50:50~52 ，2026 年營收 YOY 為 5~10%，

並表示有望達高標或是優於展望。主要成長動能為強固型電腦產品，由全球

無人機佈局方面， 2026 年無人機佔強固型電腦約 5~10% ，公司希望 2027 年
能佔 8~12% ，主要是 2026 年無人機的案子較 2025 年多 10 倍，歐美客戶都
有來接洽，未來會陸續發酵，加上台灣近期立法院剛通過的 634 億元的無人
機預算，包括為期 6 年共 442 億元的無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫，以及
192 億元的各部會無人機採購預算，故看好未來幾年神基在無人機解決方案
的成長性。

元， YOY +8.66% ；稅後純益為 60.50 億元， YOY +11.18% ；稅後 EPS 9.69
元。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

光通雷射需求伴隨著 800G 以上光收發模組大幅增長 ， 聯鈞相對應
的 COS 產能也逐年翻倍，此外子公司源傑 AOC 訂單回溫，捷敏
-KY 功率測試需求增溫，在三大事業皆美下，整體聯鈞營運表現將
顯著跳升 。 綜合以上 ， 研究部預估聯鈞2026 年 EPS 8.56 元，2027
年 EPS 16.71 元，目前 PE 為 35x，參考國內外光通訊族群 PE 區
間為 20-50x，考量公司產能逐年增加，且也將受惠高功率雷射需
求的帶動，因此給予 40xPER，目標價 650 元，建議區間操作。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
聯鈞成立於 2000 年 ， 為國內雷射封裝大廠 ， 主要產品為雷射封裝 、
AOC 以及功率元件測試 。2025 年光通訊占比 17%、 半導體62%、
矽光子 21%，銷售地區台灣 80%、中國 20%。

2 
 主要成長動能仍來自於源傑 AOC 業務的回溫。在稼動率提升下整
體獲利表現顯著改善，稅後淨利創下歷史次高表現。

封裝動能延續，AOC 訂單放量 
聯鈞 7 月營收 12.2 億元，M+7.0%，Y+98%，若單看源傑 7 月營
收貢獻 3.6 億元 ，M+4.9%，Y+1749.1%， 捷敏6.1 億元 ，M+9.1%，
Y+32.0%， 可見三大事業皆呈現顯著貢獻 ， 其中AOC 最具爆發性 。
綜合以上，研究部預估聯鈞 26Q3 營收 37.2 億元，Q+25.0%，
Y+100.6%，毛利率 33.42%，稅後淨利 3.8 億元，Q+24.4%，
Y+327.2%，EPS 2.61 元。

產能連年翻倍，高功率雷射 26Q4 有望小量貢獻 
在與客戶配合多年的經驗，聯鈞在產能擴增上都會早 先於客戶一
步 ， 因此設備上的資本支出也連年提高 ， 雷射COS 產能 2024-2026
年來也逐年翻倍。考量整體 800G 以上光收發模組於 2027 年全球
需求量將達 1 億支以上，且 200G EML 滲透率的提高，預計將帶
動 InP Laser 市場規模翻倍增長，而美、日系雷射大廠的產能也相
對應增加，可知雷射封裝需求同步提高。此外各大廠相繼於近期法
說會上指出 高功率雷射預計開始於 26Q4 小量出貨， 且將隨著
NPO/CPO 交換機的導入而高速成長， 考量高功率雷射晶片面積有
別於現有規格，封裝價格將有所提升，聯鈞也將擴充相對應的產能
因應，並隨客戶步調開始出貨。AOC 方面則在美系大客戶庫存調
整完後拉貨力道重新轉強，此外 800G 光收發模組產品也有望於
2027 年開始出貨，同樣有助於整體獲利表現的增長。功率元件的
需求則在 AI 伺服器持續往高瓦數規格升級，大廠相關的後段需求
也同步提升，同樣有利於捷敏表現。綜合以上，研究部預估聯鈞
2026 年營收 130.5 億元，Y+52.0%，毛利率 31.81%，稅後淨利
12.5 億元，Y+52.0%，EPS 8.56 元；2027 年營收 189.8 億元，
Y+45.5%，毛利率 34.41%，稅後淨利 24.3 億元，Y+95.3%，EPS 
16.71 元。

Alex.Su@yuanta.com 
劉東萌 
miketmliu@yuanta.com

Rubin 需求強勁，均熱片業務比重提升，預估毛利率將優化 
健策 2Q26 營收 72.8 億元、季增 37%，毛利率 46.8%、季增 5 個百分點，
優於我們預期 1.5 個百分點，EPS 15.8 元，優於我們預期 8%，營收及毛利
率優化主要受惠 B300 持續放量，加上 Rubin 出貨量大幅提升，帶動營收成
長，產品組合明顯優化，預估均熱片產品比重從前一季的 63%提升至本季度
的 68%。展望 3Q26，我們觀察到，Rubin 出貨量明顯優於我們原先預期，
預計出貨量 會有強勁雙位數 季成長，因此，我們預估健策 3Q26 營收 92.7
億、季增 27%，毛利率 47.5%、季增 0.7 個百分點，上修 EPS 23% 至 20.1
元、季增 27%。 
GPU 需求強勁成長，Rubin 出貨強勁延續至明年中 
我們原先預期 Rubin 今明年出貨量為 200/500 萬顆，並預期出貨高峰可能落
在 3Q26，但根據供應鏈調查，客戶對於 Rubin 產品的需求非常強勁，預期
均熱片的出貨將會持續季增至 2Q27，為健策的營收帶來強勁成長，且預期
Rubin Ultra 的 Removable Lid 產品將於 1Q27 開始小量產，2Q27 開始放量。
我們上修健策 2027 年營收 13%至 608 億元、年增 93%，毛利率 50.4%、
年增 4 個百分點，EPS 上修 23%至 149.5 元、年增 125%。 
均熱片需求不停歇，產品組合優化再優化 
我們上修健策 2026/27 年獲利 15%/23%，主要考量到高階均熱片需求強勁，
且出貨量明顯優於預期，並帶動其均熱片業務的營收比重大幅提升，毛利率
優化明確；此外，除了明年的 AMD 400 系列採用 兩件式均熱片外， AWS 
T4 晶片的規格可能將大幅提升，帶動均熱片單價再增加；另外，Rubin 目前
有在進行一次測試，可能會在未來有所進展。我們 認為健策長期趨勢仍未改
變，並維持其龍頭地位，維持健策買進評等，目標價上修至 6,725 元，基於
2027 年預估每股盈餘、45 倍本益比評價(前次採用 2027 年 x40 倍本益比)。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
2Q26 毛利率季增 5 個百分點來到 46.8%，主因 GPU 均熱片需求強
勁，產品組合明顯轉佳。 
⧫  
Rubin 出貨需求提升，且出貨量有機會持續維持高檔至 2Q27，優於原
先預期的 3Q26 高峰。 
⧫  
均熱片需求強勁，且規格持續提升，上修 目標價 至 6,725 元，基於
2027 年 EPS、45 倍本益比評價。

營運分析  
Rubin 出貨量優於預期，帶動 3Q26 營運強勁成長 
健策 2Q26 營收 72.8 億元、季增 37%，毛利率 46.8%、季增 5 個百分點，優於我們預期 1.5 個百
分點，EPS 15.8 元，優於我們預期 8%，營收及毛利率優化主要受惠 B300 持續放量，加上 Rubin
出貨量大幅 提升，帶動營收成長，產品組合明顯優化，均熱片產品比重從前一季的 63%提升至本
季度的 68%。 
展望 3Q26，我們觀察到，Rubin 出貨量明顯優於我們原先預期，預計出貨量成長會成長強勁雙位
數，因此，我們預估健策 3Q26 營收 92.7 億、季增 27%，毛利率 47.5%、季增 0.7 個百分點，上
修 EPS 23% 至 20.1 元、季增 27%。

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 8 月 20 日 Jentech 第3 頁，共 16頁  
Rubin 需求持續強勁，明年 Ultra 2Q27 開始放量 
本中心 發現 Rubin Ultra 的 均 熱 片 初 步 測 試 已 告 一 段落 ， 如 前 份 報 告 所 敘 ， 預 計 採 用 的 是
Removable Lid( 或稱為 Temporary Lid) ，其 Stiffener+Lid 採用螺絲上鎖，將會在送到 ODM 打板過
程後拆除 Lid，並放上一層散熱膏及石墨烯片，再放上水冷板，可以降低因多個 TIM 導致散熱傳遞
速度降低。 
我們原先預期 Rubin 今明年出貨量為 200/500 萬顆，並預期出貨高峰可能落在 3Q26，但根據供應
鏈調查，客戶對於 Rubin 產品的需求非常強勁，預期均熱片的出貨將會持續季增至 2Q27，為健策
的營收帶來強勁成長，且預期 Rubin Ultra 的 Removeable Lid 產品將於 1Q27 開始小量產，2Q27
開始放亮。我們上修健策 2027 年營收 13%至 608 億元、年增 93%，毛利率 50.4%、年增 4 個百
分點，EPS 上修 23%至 149.5 元、年增 125%。 
圖 3：Cold Plate + Removable Lid，可能的散熱新方式

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 8 月 20 日 Jentech 第4 頁，共 16頁  
產業概況  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 91%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic) 、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收預估約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國 CSP 資本支出將年增 91%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代表了
資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、Meta 
ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將採購
TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：散熱 2026 年 8 月 20 日 Jentech 第5 頁，共 16頁  
受惠於 xAI 強勁需求，預估下半年 GB300 出貨量明顯成長 
根據供應鏈調查，xAI 近期有對兩家組裝廠上修 GB300 訂單約 1.3 萬櫃，出貨時程將會落在 3Q26-
1Q27，其中 Supermicro 得到的訂單較為明顯，該 GB300 採用 in Rack CDU 較標準品 GB300 略
不同，需要搭配風冷背門運作(Fan Door or RDHx) 。我們上修 2026 年 GB300 出貨量至 6.8 萬櫃，
並預期 VR200 出貨量為 2,000 櫃，這次主要受惠的供應商為 Supermicro 及緯創的相關供應鏈。 
VR200 的部分，受到晶片封裝段的些微調整，出貨時程略為遞延，原先小量產時程落在 8-9 月，但
目前有調整至 9-10 月，約延後 1 個月，影響有限，我們預估今年的 VR 出貨量為 2,000 櫃；另外，
我們觀察到和碩有取得來自 xAI 的 VR200 訂單，預計 2Q27 開始出貨，出貨量可望達上千櫃。
